=== ADR 0165: AUTHORED CONTENT vs REALM POWER =========================
realm power      qi_refining -> primordial_origin : 551.5x
boss vitality    authored band        : 20.0x
so the engine's absolute damage outruns the content by 28x

=== ADR 0165: ACTOR-FACING DRIFT =====================================
spine hits-to-kill: 10.0 at R1 -> 4.0 at R30  (0.40x)

--- AUTHORED BOSS VITALITY (share model, unchanged) ---
